Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in interpolation theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 755 and the invariant I(A,B) = 129 mod 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on interpolation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 755. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 129. QED.
